### `@kb-labs/adapters-analytics-duckdb`

### `@kb-labs/adapters-analytics-file`

#### E2E / journey tests

**`adapters/analytics-file/src/__tests__/daily-stats.spec.ts`**

- **FileAnalytics - getDailyStats**
- **LLM events**
  - should aggregate LLM events by day with correct metrics
  - should filter by date range
  - should return empty array when no events match
- **Embeddings events**
  - should aggregate embeddings events with correct metrics
- **VectorStore events**
  - should count operations correctly
- **Cache events**
  - should calculate hit rate correctly
  - should handle zero gets gracefully
- **Storage events**
  - should aggregate bytes read and written
- **Sorting**
  - should sort results by date ascending
- **Empty metrics**
  - should not include metrics object for unknown event types

#### Unit tests

**`adapters/analytics-file/src/__tests__/scoped-analytics.test.ts`**

- **Analytics Source Attribution**
- **Default behavior (current implementation)**
  - should use source from AnalyticsContext
  - should use default source if no context provided
- **Multiple plugins tracking events**
  - should track events from different plugins with same analytics instance
- **Event schema validation**
  - should create events with kb.v1 schema
- **getEvents filtering by source**
  - should filter events by source.product
  - should return empty when filtering for non-existent source
- **getStats aggregation by source**
  - should aggregate events by source

**`adapters/analytics-file/src/index.test.ts`**

- **FileAnalytics - Manifest-based Context Injection**
- **Context Priority - options.workspace and options.analytics**
  - should use options.workspace when provided (manifest-based injection)
  - should use options.analytics when provided (manifest-based injection)
  - should fall back to process.cwd() when no cwd provided
  - should use "unknown" source when no analytics context provided
- **Real-world Scenario - REST API**
  - should work as in REST API with manifest-based injection

### `@kb-labs/adapters-fs`

#### Unit tests

**`adapters/fs/src/index.test.ts`**

- **FilesystemStorageAdapter**
- **Basic Operations**
  - should write and read a file
  - should return null when reading nonexistent file
  - should write file in nested directory
  - should check if file exists
  - should delete a file
  - should not throw when deleting nonexistent file
- **List Operations**
  - should list files with prefix
  - should return empty array when no files match prefix
- **Security**
  - should prevent directory traversal attacks
  - should prevent absolute path escaping baseDir
- **Extended Methods - stat()**
  - should return file metadata
  - should return null for nonexistent file
  - should detect content types correctly
  - should return octet-stream for unknown extensions
- **Extended Methods - copy()**
  - should copy file
  - should copy to nested directory
- **Extended Methods - move()**
  - should move file
  - should move to nested directory
  - should overwrite existing file when moving
- **Extended Methods - listWithMetadata()**
  - should list files with metadata
  - should return empty array when no files match
  - should include correct content types

**`adapters/fs/src/secure-storage.test.ts`**

- **SecureStorageAdapter**
- **Operation Permissions**
  - should allow read when permission granted
  - should deny read when permission not granted
  - should allow write when permission granted
  - should deny write when permission not granted
  - should allow delete when permission granted
  - should deny delete when permission explicitly set to false
- **Path Allowlist**
  - should allow access to allowlisted paths
  - should deny access to non-allowlisted paths
  - should work with empty allowlist (allow all)
  - should work with undefined allowlist (allow all)
- **Path Denylist**
  - should deny access to denylisted paths
  - should allow access to non-denylisted paths
  - should give denylist precedence over allowlist
- **List Operations with Permissions**
  - should list files respecting permissions
  - should deny list for non-allowlisted paths
- **Exists with Permissions**
  - should check existence when allowed
  - should return false for denied paths (security by obscurity)
- **Extended Methods with Permissions**
  - should allow stat when permitted
  - should deny stat when not permitted
  - should allow copy when both paths permitted
  - should deny copy when source not permitted
  - should deny copy when destination not permitted
  - should allow move when both paths permitted
  - should allow listWithMetadata when permitted
- **Complex Permission Scenarios**
  - should handle multiple allowlist patterns
  - should handle multiple denylist patterns
  - should combine allowlist and denylist correctly
  - should handle read-only permissions

### `@kb-labs/adapters-service-transport-http`

#### Unit tests

**`adapters/service-transport-http/src/__tests__/offset.test.ts`**

- **applyPortOffset**
  - shifts the TCP port, preserving scheme/host
  - is a no-op for offset 0
  - is a no-op for urls without an explicit port (socket placeholders)
  - returns the input unchanged when unparseable
- **connectionInfo with offset**
  - shifts TCP baseUrl by the offset
  - leaves socket services untouched (route via socketPath)
  - defaults offset to 0 when neither config nor env set
- **listenAddress (bind)**
  - returns shifted port; bind host independent of route host
  - returns socketPath for socket services (offset is a no-op)
  - returns undefined for a TCP service whose url has no explicit port
  - returns undefined for unknown service

**`adapters/service-transport-http/src/__tests__/transport.test.ts`**

- **connectionInfo()**
  - returns { baseUrl, socketPath } for unix config
  - returns { baseUrl, socketPath: undefined } for TCP config
  - returns undefined for unknown serviceId
  - throws when socketPath contains an unresolved ${...} placeholder
- **call()**
  - makes GET request when no payload
  - makes POST request when payload present
  - overrides HTTP method via metadata["http-method"]
  - ok: false for 404 response
  - throws on unknown serviceId
  - propagates AbortSignal — aborted request throws
- **stream()**
  - returns AsyncIterable body without buffering
  - AsyncIterable yields all chunks
  - throws on unknown serviceId
- **health()**
  - returns { status: ok } when all services /health → 200
  - returns { status: degraded } when a service is unreachable
  - individual failure does not throw — captured in results map
- **listenAddress()**
  - returns { socketPath } when socketPath is configured
  - returns { port } for TCP service
  - returns undefined for unknown serviceId
  - throws when socketPath contains an unresolved ${...} placeholder

**`adapters/service-transport-http/src/__tests__/unix-socket.test.ts`**

- **unix socket mode**
  - call() connects via socketPath and returns response
  - call() POST with payload works over socket
  - stream() yields chunks from a streaming endpoint via socket
  - concurrent calls over same pool all succeed
- **AbortSignal on unix socket**
  - aborted signal cancels the request
- **service restart resilience**
  - pool reconnects after service restart at same socket path

### `@kb-labs/agent-entry`

#### E2E / journey tests

**`plugins/agents/entry/tests/turn-reducer.spec.ts`**

- **turn-reducer — applyDelta**
  - applies turn:created and advances lastSeq
  - ignores a delta whose seq is <= lastSeq (idempotent replay, e.g. on reconnect)
  - flags a gap when seq skips ahead of lastSeq + 1, but still applies it
  - turn:step:appended adds a new step without touching existing ones
  - turn:step:updated replaces the step with matching id, leaves other steps alone
  - turn:status updates status/completedAt/error without touching steps
  - turn:metadata merges the patch into existing metadata, not a full replace
  - a delta for an unknown turnId is a silent no-op on turns (but still advances lastSeq via gap/applied)
- **turn-reducer — setTurn / advanceSeq**
  - setTurn inserts/replaces a turn directly without requiring a seq
  - advanceSeq only moves the cursor forward, never backward
- **turn-reducer — reconcileOptimistic**
  - drops an optimistic turn once a real turn with the same clientId appears
  - leaves unrelated optimistic turns alone

  - shadowed intruder command is not routed — first owner wins
  - alias pointing into an already-owned group is blocked
  - undefined packageName is treated as __unknown__ and can still own a namespace

- **Canonical ID Strategy**
  - bare plugin (no group): canonical = id
  - 2-part plugin (group:id): canonical = group id
  - 3-part plugin (group:subgroup:id): canonical = group subgroup id
- **2-Part Shorthand for 3-Part Commands**
  - suggests ["marketplace", "list"] as "did you mean" for 3-part command (unambiguous)
  - getCommandAt does NOT resolve 2-part shorthand (exact lookup only)
  - getCommandAt resolves via full canonical segments
- **User-Defined Aliases**
  - resolves manifest aliases to the correct plugin
  - getCommandAt retrieves by canonical segments
- **Collision: System Always Wins**
  - system command beats plugin when canonical paths match exactly
  - system bare command does NOT shadow plugin in different group
  - system command beats plugin with same path
  - system group beats plugin with same name
  - logs warning when plugin collides with system command (same canonical)
  - shadowed plugin is stored in listCommands but not routed
  - plugin alias that collides with system command is blocked
- **Group Routing**
  - registered group returns type=system-group
  - subcommand within group resolves to system-cmd
  - listCommandsUnder returns plugin commands under a group
- **Command Namespace Routing**
  - prefers a multi-word command over a bare command namespace
  - falls back to the bare command when the next token is an argument
- **Multiple Plugins in Same Group**
  - registers multiple commands under same group:subgroup
  - listCommands returns all unique plugins
  - listCommandsUnder returns commands in that group
- **Cross-Group Bare ID (no false collisions)**
  - different groups with same bare id do not collide
  - system group bare subcommand does not shadow plugin with same bare id in different group
  - system bare command "status" does not shadow plugin group:status
  - multiple plugins with same bare id are all listed in listCommands
- **Edge Cases**
  - returns not-found for unknown command
  - getCommandAt returns non-null for registered plugin (full path)
  - getCommandAt returns null for 2-part shorthand (exact lookup only)
  - getCommandAt returns null for unknown command
  - listCommands returns no duplicates for multiply-keyed commands
  - getCommandAt retrieves by canonical segments

**`core/config/src/__tests__/effective-config.spec.ts`**

- **loadEffectiveConfig — layer presence matrix**
  - returns null when no layer contributes
  - platform-only (project missing, no overlays)
  - project-only (no platformRoot)
  - overlays-only (no platform, no project)
  - platform + project (project overrides platform)
  - platform + overlays (no project file)
  - project + overlays (no platformRoot)
  - all three layers merged in order
- **loadEffectiveConfig — layer ordering**
  - overlays win over project layer
  - project wins over platform layer
  - overlays win over platform even without a project layer
  - later overlay (by lex order) wins over earlier overlay
  - overlayPaths reflects lex application order
  - numeric prefixes sort lexically (not numerically)
- **loadEffectiveConfig — deep-merge semantics**
  - nested objects merge recursively
  - introduced keys are added (no shadowing siblings)
  - platform↔project arrays CONCATENATE (mergeDefined semantics, same as loadPlatformConfig)
  - overlay arrays REPLACE base arrays by default
  - kb:merge:append concatenates arrays
  - kb:merge directive key is stripped from the result
  - multiple overlays each append (cumulative)
  - type mismatch: overlay value wins (object → scalar)
  - type mismatch: overlay value wins (scalar → object)
  - null in overlay overrides defined value
  - empty overlay object is a no-op
- **loadEffectiveConfig — file format precedence**
  - .kb/kb.config.json wins over .kb/kb.config.jsonc when both exist
  - falls through to root-level kb.config.jsonc when .kb/ absent
  - .kb/ files win over root-level files in the same root (precedence)
  - parses JSONC with line comments and trailing commas
  - deep-merges BOTH .kb/kb.config.jsonc and .kb/kb.config.json when both exist (.json wins)
  - parses JSONC overlays with comments
- **loadEffectiveConfig — diagnostics**
  - reports JSON_PARSE_FAILED for malformed project config
  - reports CONFIG_NOT_OBJECT when project top-level is an array
  - reports CONFIG_NOT_OBJECT when platform top-level is an array
  - reports OVERLAY_NOT_OBJECT for non-object overlay
  - skips malformed overlay but applies the rest
  - emits no diagnostics on the happy path
  - aggregates diagnostics across multiple layers
  - empty overlay file is reported as JSON_PARSE_FAILED, not silent
- **loadEffectiveConfig — path resolution & result fields**
  - returns absolute paths in all path fields
  - ignores platformRoot when it equals projectRoot (no double-merge)
  - treats undefined platformRoot as no platform layer
  - platformConfigPath unset when platform root has no kb.config.*
  - projectConfigPath unset when project has only overlays
  - result is null only when literally nothing exists
- **loadEffectiveConfig — overlay isolation**
  - overlays placed under platformRoot are NOT loaded
  - non-.jsonc files in overlays/ are ignored
  - overlays in a subdirectory of .kb/overlays/ are NOT loaded
- **loadEffectiveConfig — realistic scenarios**
  - gateway pressure: overlay adds pressure section to full platform baseline
  - overlay disables a baseline-enabled feature
  - overlay extends an adapter list via kb:merge:append
  - two overlays cooperate (one tweaks baseline, another adds new field)
- **loadEffectiveConfig — idempotency & side effects**
  - two consecutive loads return equal data without mutating disk
  - mutating the returned data does not affect subsequent calls

  - requires id, kind, and authenticate

- **IIdentityProvider union (DD-1)**
  - narrows by `kind` — only the redirect member has startAuthorization
  - exposes authenticate(input: unknown) on the union WITHOUT narrowing (DD-1)

  - IdentityUserPort.findByEmailTenant returns a nullable IdentityUserRecord
  - IdentityCredentialPort.getCredential returns a nullable IdentityCredentialRecord

- **IPolicyDecisionPoint contract**
  - exposes check and enumeratePermissions
  - PolicyDecision is a discriminated union on `allow`
- **Identity shape**
  - carries userId, tenantId, and type (user|machine)
  - does NOT include role, scopes, or permissions (CD-7, ADR principle 3)
- **Resource and PolicyContext shapes**
  - Resource has type, optional id and tenantId

**`core/resource-broker/src/retry/__tests__/retry-strategy.test.ts`**

- **calculateBackoffDelay**
  - attempt 0 → baseDelay * 2^0 = baseDelay
  - attempt 1 → baseDelay * 2^1 = 2000
  - attempt 2 → baseDelay * 2^2 = 4000
  - caps at maxDelay
  - retryAfterHint overrides exponential backoff
  - retryAfterHint is capped at maxDelay
  - retryAfterHint=0 is ignored (falls back to backoff)
  - jitter increases delay within expected range
- **shouldRetry**
  - retryable error + attempts left → shouldRetry=true
  - non-retryable error → shouldRetry=false
  - retryable error but no attempts left → shouldRetry=false
  - rate_limit uses at least 5s base delay
  - delayMs=0 when not retrying
  - uses DEFAULT_RETRY_CONFIG when no config given
- **withRetry**
  - succeeds on first attempt
  - retries and succeeds on 3rd attempt
  - throws last error after all retries exhausted
  - stops immediately on non-retryable error
- **createRateLimitRetryConfig**
  - defaults to maxRetries=5, baseDelay=5000
  - accepts custom maxRetries
- **createQuickRetryConfig**
  - defaults to maxRetries=3, baseDelay=500
  - does not include rate_limit (quick ops should not wait 5s)

### `@kb-labs/core-workspace`

#### E2E / journey tests

**`core/workspace/src/workspace/__tests__/root-resolver.spec.ts`**

- **resolveProjectRoot**
  - returns explicit cwd when provided
  - prefers KB_PROJECT_ROOT when set
  - accepts legacy KB_LABS_WORKSPACE_ROOT for backwards compatibility
  - accepts legacy KB_LABS_REPO_ROOT for backwards compatibility
  - finds nearest kb-labs config ancestor
  - falls back to repo root when config missing
  - returns fallback when no markers found

  - behaves identically to resolveProjectRoot
- **resolvePlatformRoot**
  - returns explicit cwd when provided
  - prefers KB_PLATFORM_ROOT env var
  - resolves platform root via moduleUrl when node_modules/@kb-labs/cli-bin is present
  - walks up from startDir to find node_modules/@kb-labs/cli-bin
  - walks up from startDir to find pnpm-workspace.yaml (dev mode)
  - prefers pnpm-workspace.yaml above a nested package marker (dev-mode correction)
  - prefers top-most pnpm-workspace.yaml in nested-workspace layouts
  - ignores platform markers inside a pnpm virtual store
  - falls back to repo root when no markers match
- **resolveRoots**
  - returns sameLocation: true in dev-mode workspace (both resolve to same root)
  - returns sameLocation: false in installed mode
  - env vars override discovery for both roots independently

#### E2E / journey tests

**`e2e/auth/specs-oauth/08-oauth.spec.ts`**

- shows a "Continue with corp" button discovered from /auth/providers
- completes the full authorization-code flow and lands authenticated
- session cookies set on the callback carry the Secure + HttpOnly attributes
- unknown / non-redirect provider on /start → 400
- callback with no matching state cookie → bounced to /login?error

- AUTH-01: cold visit without cookie → redirected to /login
- AUTH-02: bootstrap login → authenticated, correct cookie attributes
- AUTH-03: logout → cookies cleared, subsequent /auth/me returns 401
- AUTH-04: wrong credentials → error message, no session cookies
- AUTH-CD4: email canonicalization — login with uppercase/padded email succeeds

**`e2e/auth/specs/02-session-lifecycle.spec.ts`**

- AUTH-05: access token expiry → transparent refresh → continues working
- AUTH-06: refresh token expiry → redirected to /login
- AUTH-07a: refresh grace window — parallel rotation within grace → both succeed
- AUTH-07b: reuse detection — reuse old jti after grace window → 401
- AUTH-08: multi-device — logout in one context does not affect the other
- AUTH-09: revoke session → revoked browser gets 401 on next refresh

**`e2e/auth/specs/03-cross-tenant.spec.ts`**

- AUTH-10: cross-tenant guard — access token for tenant A rejected on tenant B endpoint
- AUTH-11: cookie isolation — cookies are origin-scoped, no Domain= attribute

- AUTH-12: admin creates invite → gets activation URL in response

- AUTH-15: reused invite (already consumed) → "invalid or expired" message
- AUTH-16: activation page has Referrer-Policy=no-referrer meta tag

**`e2e/auth/specs/05-admin-flows.spec.ts`**

- AUTH-17: disable user mid-session → next request returns 401 (CD-1)
- AUTH-18: enable disabled user → fresh login works
- AUTH-19: member cannot create invites → 403 Forbidden

**`e2e/auth/specs/06-password-change.spec.ts`**

- AUTH-20: change password → other device signed out, current session lives
- AUTH-21: change password with wrong current → 400 error
- AUTH-22: new password < 8 chars → client-side validation error

- AUTH-24: HIBP unavailable → password change still succeeds (graceful)

**`e2e/auth/specs/07-security.spec.ts`**

- AUTH-25: missing X-CSRF-Token on mutating endpoint → 403
- AUTH-26: mismatched X-CSRF-Token → 403
- AUTH-27: rate limit per-IP — 11th failed login within 1m → 429
- AUTH-28: rate limit per-email — 6th failed login for same email → 429
- AUTH-29: cookie attributes — HttpOnly/Secure/SameSite=Strict/no-Domain
- AUTH-30: Bearer auth (machine client) succeeds without CSRF header
- AUTH-31: /api/auth/me response contains no role field (CD-3)

### `@kb-labs/e2e-oidc-idp`

#### E2E / journey tests

**`e2e/oidc-idp/src/__tests__/idp-server.test.ts`**

- **e2e OIDC IdP server**
  - serves a discovery document with the expected shape
  - publishes a single RS256 signing key via JWKS
  - renders a login form on the first /authorize hop (no email)
  - rejects /authorize with an unknown client_id
  - rejects /authorize with a redirect_uri outside the allow-list
  - completes the authorization-code flow and echoes the nonce
  - binds iss/aud so the RP can verify against its configured issuer
  - rejects token exchange with the wrong client_secret (401 invalid_client)
  - treats authorization codes as single-use
  - rejects token exchange when redirect_uri does not match the code
  - enforces PKCE S256 when a code_challenge was supplied
  - accepts a correct PKCE verifier
  - rejects an unknown grant_type

#### WebSocket integration tests

**`services/gateway/app/src/ws/__tests__/gateway-ws.test.ts`**

- **pickSocketWsUpstream**
  - matches a path under the prefix
  - matches the prefix exactly
  - does NOT match a prefix that is only a string-prefix, not a path boundary
  - returns undefined when no upstream matches
  - returns undefined for an empty upstream list
- **buildUpstreamWsUrl**
  - builds ws+unix://<socket>:<path> preserving the path under prefix
  - rewrites the prefix when rewritePrefix differs
  - preserves the query string
- **forwardWsHeaders**
  - forwards auth / correlation / subprotocol / cookie headers
  - omits headers that are absent

**`services/gateway/app/src/ws/__tests__/pump.test.ts`**

- **pumpBidirectional**
  - forwards a text frame client → upstream (after open)
  - forwards a text frame upstream → client
  - preserves the binary flag in both directions
  - buffers client frames sent BEFORE upstream open, flushes on open
  - propagates upstream close(code, reason) to the client
  - closes upstream when the client closes
  - closes both sides with 1011 on upstream error
  - is idempotent: a second close does not re-close
  - writes one correlated relay summary with frame counters
  - does not send to a non-OPEN peer

**`services/gateway/app/src/ws/__tests__/ws-unix-proxy.integration.test.ts`**

- **gateway WS-over-unix dialer (integration)**
  - opens a connection through the gateway to a socket-bound upstream
  - relays a text frame round-trip
  - relays a binary frame round-trip intact
  - propagates an upstream-initiated close code to the client
  - closes the client cleanly when the upstream socket does not exist (no hang)
  - keeps two concurrent connections isolated

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/access-modes.e2e.test.ts`**

- secured install: the admin the installer configured can log in, everyone else cannot

- local install: no login needed, and the gateway is unreachable from other machines
- secured install stays reachable on all interfaces (control for the local check)

- **Gateway auth errors**

- **Gateway auth errors > missing Authorization header**
  - GET /hosts → 401
- **malformed Authorization header**
  - plain token without "Bearer " prefix → 401
  - empty Bearer value → 401
- **invalid JWT**

  - GET /hosts with wrong-signature JWT → 401
- **invalid client credentials**
  - POST /auth/token with unknown clientId → 401
  - POST /auth/token with wrong secret for real clientId → 401
- **invalid refresh token**
  - POST /auth/refresh with garbage refreshToken → 401

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/gateway-cancel.e2e.test.ts`**

- **Gateway execution cancel**
  - POST /api/v1/execute/:id/cancel → execution:done(exitCode=130)
  - POST /api/v1/execute/:id/cancel with nonexistent id → 4xx
  - POST /api/v1/execute/:id/cancel without auth → 401

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/live-gateway.e2e.test.ts`**

- returns { status: healthy|degraded }
- POST /auth/register returns clientId + clientSecret + hostId
- POST /auth/token returns accessToken + refreshToken
- POST /auth/token returns 401 for wrong credentials
- POST /auth/refresh rotates refresh token
- JWT token is accepted on protected endpoint GET /hosts
- static dev-studio-token is accepted on protected endpoint
- GET /hosts returns 401 without token
- POST /hosts/register returns hostId + machineToken
- host WS handshake: hello → connected (machineToken auth)
- host goes online in registry after WS handshake
- heartbeat gets ack response
- WS closes with 1008 if no Authorization header
- streams execution:done(exitCode=0) when host responds to call
- streams execution:error + execution:done(exitCode=1) when host returns error
- returns 503 when no host is connected for namespace
- returns 401 without token
- returns 400 for missing required fields
- cancels an in-flight execution and streams execution:cancelled + done(exitCode=130)
- client receives execution events via WS subscription
- client:subscribe to nonexistent execution returns client:error
- client:cancel cancels execution and aborts host call

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/ws-client-connect.e2e.test.ts`**

- **WebSocket /clients/connect: auth rejection**
  - closes immediately when no Authorization header
  - closes with 1008 for invalid token
- **WebSocket /clients/connect: handshake**
  - completes client:hello → client:connected flow
  - closes with 1008 if client:hello not sent within timeout
  - closes with 1008 if client:hello has invalid format
  - access_token query param is accepted as fallback auth
- **WebSocket /clients/connect: subscribe / unsubscribe**
  - receives client:error(EXECUTION_NOT_FOUND) when subscribing to unknown execution
  - receives client:error(FORBIDDEN) when subscribing to execution in different namespace
  - does not error on unsubscribe from unknown execution (graceful)
  - receives client:error(INVALID_MESSAGE) for malformed JSON
  - receives client:error(INVALID_MESSAGE) for unknown message type
- **WebSocket /clients/connect: cancel**
  - receives client:error(EXECUTION_NOT_FOUND) when cancelling unknown execution
  - cancels execution and aborts the signal
  - receives client:error(CANCEL_FAILED) when execution already cancelled

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/ws-handshake.e2e.test.ts`**

- **WebSocket: connection refused without auth**
  - closes immediately when no Authorization header
  - closes with 1008 for invalid machine token
- **WebSocket: full handshake**
  - completes hello → connected flow
  - heartbeat gets ack response
  - rejects unsupported protocol version
  - closes with 1008 if hello not sent within timeout
- **WebSocket: host status lifecycle**
  - host goes online after successful handshake

#### Unit tests

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/access.test.ts`**

- **nothing set — behaviour before access.mode existed**
  - login required, deployed-platform bind
- **access.mode**
  - secured: login required, deployed bind
  - local: no login and loopback bind by default
  - rejects an unknown mode instead of guessing
- **the trap the schema default would have created**
  - local + an auth object that only carries bootstrap identity stays local
  - secured + auth.bootstrap keeps login required
- **explicit operator choices win over access.mode**
  - auth.enabled: true beats local (contradiction resolves to the safe side)
  - auth.enabled: false beats secured
  - explicit host beats the mode-implied bind
- **B-023 guardrail inputs (auth off must be loopback)**
  - local with no host is fine — it now binds loopback instead of being refused
  - local with an explicit public host still violates
  - legacy auth.enabled:false with no host is still refused (unchanged)
  - secured never violates, whatever the host
- **resolveBootstrapTenantId — config, then env, then default**
  - config wins over the env
  - the env is used when config does not name a tenant
  - falls back to the platform default
  - a bootstrap block without a tenant is valid config (an installer may write only the admin email)

  - returns true and aborts signal

  - returns false when already cancelled (idempotent)

- **remove()**
  - removes execution from registry

- **get()**
  - returns undefined for unknown id

- **cancelByHost()**
  - cancels all executions for given hostId
  - returns empty array when no executions for host
  - skips already-aborted executions
  - returns executionIds of cancelled executions
- **size**
  - starts at 0
  - tracks additions and removals correctly
- **CancelledError**
  - stores reason
  - has descriptive message
  - has correct name
  - is instanceof Error
  - is instanceof CancelledError
  - supports all cancellation reasons

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/registry.test.ts`**

- **HostRegistry.register**
  - creates a new host descriptor with offline status
  - stores descriptor and token in cache
  - generates unique hostId and token per registration
- **HostRegistry.setOnline / setOffline**
  - sets status to online and adds connectionId
  - deduplicates connections on repeated setOnline
  - each setOnline replaces connections with the new connectionId
  - enters reconnecting status when last connection removed (grace period)
  - stays online when disconnected connectionId is not the current one
  - does nothing if host not found
- **HostRegistry.heartbeat**
  - updates lastSeen timestamp
- **HostRegistry.get**
  - returns host descriptor by id
  - returns null for unknown host
- **HostRegistry.resolveToken**
  - returns hostId/namespaceId for valid machine token
  - returns null for unknown token
- **HostRegistry with IHostStore (dual-layer)**
- **HostRegistry with IHostStore (dual-layer) > register**
  - writes to both cache and store
- **restore**
  - loads all hosts from store into cache as offline
  - returns 0 when store is empty
  - returns 0 when no store provided
- **get (cache miss → store fallback)**
  - falls through to store when cache misses
  - returns null when both cache and store miss
  - prefers cache over store
- **resolveToken (cache miss → store fallback)**
  - falls through to store when cache misses
- **list (store authoritative)**
  - uses store as source of truth, enriches with cache status
- **deregister**
  - removes from both cache and store
  - returns false for non-existent host
  - returns true and clears cache when no persistent store (cache-only mode)
- **ensureRegistered**
  - persists to store when host does not exist
  - updates capabilities in store when they change
- **HostRegistry grace period**
  - sets status to reconnecting on last connection close
  - cancels grace timer when host reconnects before expiry
  - transitions to offline after grace period expires
  - stays online when other connections remain

  - falls back to hardcoded defaults when config.auth is entirely absent and no env override
  - AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE only disables when exactly "false" — any other value keeps config/default
  - env vars also win when config.auth is absent (env-only override, e.g. no bootstrap block written yet)

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/retry-executor.test.ts`**

- **executeWithRetry — success paths**
  - returns result on first attempt
  - succeeds after one failure when maxAttempts=2
  - returns value from later attempt
- **executeWithRetry — exhausted retries**
  - throws last error after maxAttempts exhausted
  - emits execution:retry event between attempts
  - does not retry non-retryable errors by default (onlyRetryable=true)
  - retries non-retryable when onlyRetryable=false
- **executeWithRetry — cancellation**
  - throws CancelledError immediately if signal is already aborted
  - throws CancelledError when signal aborts during dispatch
  - aborts during retry backoff delay
  - does not retry after CancelledError
- **executeWithRetry — retryable error classification**
  - retries on: "${msg}"
  - does not retry on: "${msg}"
- **executeWithRetry — maxAttempts edge cases**
  - treats maxAttempts=0 as 1 attempt
  - default config (undefined) is single attempt

**`services/gateway/app/src/__tests__/subscription-registry.test.ts`**

- **SubscriptionRegistry — subscribe / unsubscribe**
  - subscribe adds to both indexes
  - multiple connections can subscribe to same execution
  - one connection can subscribe to multiple executions
  - subscribe is idempotent
  - unsubscribe removes from both indexes
  - unsubscribe GCs empty sets
  - unsubscribe only removes one connection, other subscribers remain
  - unsubscribe on non-existent connection/execution does not throw
- **SubscriptionRegistry — registerSocket / removeSocket**
  - registerSocket stores socket for broadcast
  - removeSocket prevents broadcast after removal
- **SubscriptionRegistry — broadcast**
  - broadcasts to all open subscribers
  - does not broadcast to closed sockets
  - does not broadcast to connections without a registered socket
  - broadcast sends JSON-serialised event
  - broadcast to execution with no subscribers is a no-op
  - broadcast only reaches subscribers of the target execution
- **SubscriptionRegistry — removeConnection**
  - returns orphaned executionIds (those with zero subscribers after removal)
  - does not return executionId still subscribed by another connection
  - returns empty array if connection had no subscriptions
  - cleans up all indexes for removed connection
  - removes socket during removeConnection
  - returns orphaned list with multiple executions
- **SubscriptionRegistry — metrics**
  - connectionCount tracks unique connections with subscriptions
  - subscriptionCount is the sum of all (connection, execution) pairs
  - counts decrease after unsubscribe
  - counts reset to 0 after all connections removed

  - valid HMAC → { valid: true }
  - tampered body → { valid: false }

- **verifyWebhookAuth — custom**

  - validator returns { valid: false } → { valid: false }
  - validator throws → { valid: false, reason: "validator error" }
  - no secret entry → { valid: false, reason: "not provisioned" }

  - moves current to previous with 24h expiry
  - second rotate replaces current and keeps new previous
  - persists updated entry to cache
- **delete**
  - removes the entry
  - delete is idempotent (no error if not found)
- **multi-instance key isolation**
  - instance key is separate from no-instance key
  - deleting one instance does not affect others

**`services/gateway/app/src/auth/__tests__/health-route.test.ts`**

- **GET /health/auth — served**
  - returns the readiness to a local operator without a token
- **GET /health/auth — denied with a plain 404 (never 401/403, never the body)**
  - guard 1: non-loopback TCP peer
  - guard 1: a spoofed X-Forwarded-For claiming loopback does not help a remote peer
  - guard 3: loopback peer but the public Host that a proxy forwards
  - guard 3: a host that merely starts like a loopback name is not loopback
- **isLocalOperatorRequest**
  - is false without a socket address
  - is false without a Host header
  - is true for a local, unproxied, loopback-Host request
- **logAuthReadiness (startup)**
  - logs an error with code and hint for a locked-out install
  - logs warnings at warn level and does not say "ok" while issues remain
  - logs a single info line when everything is fine
  - never throws: a failing check degrades to a warning

**`services/gateway/app/src/auth/__tests__/oauth-return-to.test.ts`**

- **validateReturnTo**
  - accepts a same-origin relative path
  - rejects absolute URLs (open redirect)
  - rejects protocol-relative URLs
  - rejects backslash-tricks browsers normalise to //
  - rejects non-string / empty / non-leading-slash input

**`services/gateway/app/src/auth/__tests__/oauth-routes.test.ts`**

- **GET /auth/oauth/:id/start**
  - 302s to the upstream IdP with state, sets a Lax state cookie, persists state in KV
  - forces an open-redirect returnTo down to "/" (Step 4b)
  - 400s for an unknown provider
  - 400s for a password provider (not a redirect provider)
- **GET /auth/oauth/:id/callback**
  - valid state + good code → sets session cookies and 302s to returnTo
  - missing state → redirect to /login?error
  - unknown/forged state → redirect to /login?error
  - replayed state (one-shot) → second callback rejected
  - cross-tenant: state minted for tenant A, callback Host of tenant B → rejected
  - state-cookie binding: query state without matching cookie → rejected (Step 4b)
  - provider mix-up: URL :id differs from state.providerId → rejected (Step 4b)
  - IdP error param → redirect to /login?error without touching the provider
  - auth failure (no user) → redirect to /login?error, no session cookies
- **OAuth per-IP rate-limit (Step 4b)**
  - blocks callback floods from one IP with 429
  - blocks start floods from one IP with 429

**`services/gateway/app/src/auth/__tests__/user-auth-middleware.test.ts`**

- **no cookie present**
  - does not set authContext; downstream is free to authenticate by Bearer
- **happy path**
  - sets authContext to a user with userId, tenantId, familyId
- **bad cookie**
  - returns 401 for a malformed JWT (does NOT fall through)
  - returns 401 for a JWT signed with a different secret
- **CD-1: middleware checks User.status on every request**
  - rejects a request from a disabled user even if access token is still valid
  - rejects when the user no longer exists
- **cross-tenant guard**
  - rejects a cookie issued for tenant A presented on tenant B subdomain
  - allows request when host resolves to null (reserved subdomain / localhost)
  - allows request from localhost (E2E / dev — no subdomain routing)
- **Bearer user token (CLI session — no cookie involved)**
  - a valid user access token via Authorization: Bearer sets authContext identically to the cookie path
  - an invalid Bearer value falls through silently (no cookie present → not a hard 401)
  - a machine-shaped Bearer token (type !== "user") falls through instead of being misidentified as a user token
  - a cookie takes precedence over a simultaneously-present Bearer token
  - CD-1 still applies over the Bearer path: a disabled user is rejected
  - cross-tenant guard still applies over the Bearer path

**`services/gateway/app/src/auth/__tests__/user-routes.test.ts`**

- **POST /auth/login**
  - returns 200 and sets three session cookies on success
  - returns 401 with { error: "invalid_credentials" } on wrong password (CD-8)
  - returns 401 with identical shape on unknown email (CD-8)
  - returns 400 when email is missing
  - returns 400 when password is missing
  - falls back to bootstrapTenantId when Host resolves no tenant and body carries none (direct/local gateway access, e.g. localhost:4000 — regression for the kb-create bootstrap-admin login bug)
- **POST /auth/login/cli**
  - returns 200 with tokens in the body and sets NO cookies
  - returns 401 with { error: "invalid_credentials" } on wrong password (same shape as /auth/login)
  - returns 400 when email or password is missing
  - falls back to bootstrapTenantId when Host resolves no tenant (regression for `kb auth login` against a direct/local gateway URL)
- **POST /auth/refresh/cli**
  - rotates the token pair given a valid refreshToken in the body, sets no cookies
  - returns 400 when refreshToken is missing
  - returns 401 for an invalid/tampered refreshToken
- **Bearer user token (from /auth/login/cli) → POST /auth/register**
  - an admin session token succeeds via Bearer, exactly like the cookie path
  - a non-admin member session token gets 403 (PDP denies MACHINE_REGISTER)
- **POST /auth/logout**
  - clears session cookies on logout with valid CSRF
  - returns 401 when no session cookie is present
  - returns 403 when CSRF token header is missing
  - returns 403 when CSRF token does not match cookie value
- **POST /auth/refresh (user cookie path)**
  - rotates the refresh cookie and issues new access + refresh cookies
  - falls back to machine body path (400) when no cookie and no body
  - returns 401 for a tampered refresh cookie
- **GET /auth/me (user context)**
  - returns userId, email, tenantId for cookie-auth user
  - does NOT include role or group fields in the response
  - returns 401 without any auth
  - returns a Studio-compatible shape (email, tenantId) under local/no-auth mode
- **GET /auth/providers**
  - returns list of registered providers without auth
- **GET /auth/permissions**
  - returns permissions list for authenticated admin
  - returns 401 without cookie
  - returns all permissions for LOCAL_ADMIN machine context (auth disabled)
- **POST /auth/activate**
  - activates invite and auto-logs in (sets session cookies)
  - returns 401 for an invalid token
  - returns 400 when token is missing
- **POST /auth/password/change**
  - changes password successfully with CSRF
  - returns 400 when current password is wrong
  - returns 401 without session cookie
  - returns 403 without CSRF token
- **GET /auth/sessions**
  - returns session list including the current session
- **POST /auth/sessions/:fam/revoke**
  - revokes a target session family
  - returns 403 without CSRF token
- **POST /auth/sessions/revoke-all**
  - revokes all sessions except current
- **POST /auth/invites (admin only)**
  - creates an invite and returns activationUrl (admin)
  - returns 403 for a non-admin member
- **GET /auth/invites (admin only)**
  - returns invite list for admin
- **GET /auth/users (admin only)**
  - returns user list including admin for tenant
- **POST /auth/users/:id/disable (admin only, CD-1)**
  - disables user and their next request with valid access token returns 401 (CD-1)
- **POST /auth/users/:id/enable (admin only)**
  - re-enables a disabled user
- **POST /auth/invites/:id/revoke (admin only)**
  - revokes a pending invite
- **admin endpoints — cross-tenant isolation**
  - disable: admin of tenant A cannot disable a user in tenant B → 404, target untouched
  - enable: admin of tenant A cannot enable a user in tenant B → 404, target untouched
  - invite revoke: admin of tenant A cannot revoke an invite in tenant B → 404, invite stays active
  - disable: returns 404 for a non-existent user id (no existence leak)
- **POST /auth/login — rate limiting**
  - returns 429 once the per-email failure window is exhausted
  - does not throttle a successful login (only failures count)

**`services/gateway/app/src/docs/routes.test.ts`**

- **GET /openapi-merged.json**
  - excludes a 503 "not ready" upstream from the merge instead of treating its error body as a spec
  - returns a valid merged doc, not a bare error body, when only one upstream answers and it is not ready
  - does not cache a degraded merge computed from zero real specs
  - caches a merge that includes at least one real spec

**`services/gateway/app/src/pressure/__tests__/hooks.test.ts`**

- **createPressureOnRequest**
  - does nothing when pressure is not configured
  - allows when under limit and registers a release handle
  - returns 429 with Retry-After when over limit
  - skips WS upgrade requests
- **createPressurePreHandler (per-tenant)**
  - skips when perTenant is disabled
  - skips when authContext is missing
  - lazy-registers a tenant resource on first request and reuses it on second
  - returns 429 with tenant layer when over limit
  - isolates tenants — tenant B is not affected by tenant A exhausting its limit
- **createPressureOnResponse**
  - releases every queued handle exactly once
  - is a noop when no releases are queued
  - integration — onRequest → onResponse releases the broker slot
  - aborted connection close fires release as well (double-release is idempotent)

**`services/gateway/app/src/pressure/__tests__/register-limits.test.ts`**

- **registerPressureLimits**
  - registers nothing when config is undefined
  - registers nothing when pressure.enabled is false
  - registers one resource per perService entry
  - registers one resource per perRoute override
  - reports perTenantEnabled when perTenant.enabled is true
  - is idempotent — second call does not throw and resource stays registered
  - emits a single pressure.boot info log

**`services/gateway/app/src/pressure/__tests__/resolve.test.ts`**

- **resolveResourceId**
  - returns null when pressure config is absent
  - returns null when pressure.enabled is false
  - resolves to a per-service resource via upstream prefix match
  - returns null when upstream matches but perService is empty for it
  - matches a per-route override over a per-service one
  - filters per-route override by HTTP method when methods are set
  - without methods restriction matches any HTTP method
  - returns the first matching override when multiple match
  - ignores query string when matching prefix

### `@kb-labs/gateway-auth`

#### Unit tests

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/auth-readiness.test.ts`**

- **auth enabled**
  - is ok with one active admin and a real secret
  - empty database → no_active_admin error with a copy-pasteable fix
  - falls back to a placeholder email in the hint when none is configured
  - a disabled admin does not count
  - a tenant-member does not count as an admin
  - an admin of another tenant does not count
  - a dangling membership (user deleted) does not count and does not throw
  - counts several active admins
- **bootstrap status**
  - failed → bootstrap_failed error, even when an admin exists
  - exists-non-active → warning, plus the error when that leaves no admin
- **jwt secret**
  - default secret on a reachable bind is an error
  - default secret on loopback is only a warning (dev)
- **auth disabled (solo/local)**
  - never reports issues, even with no admin and a default secret
- **the seeded-by-bootstrap path**
  - a bootstrap-provisioned admin makes a fresh install ready
  - never leaks secret material in its output

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/bootstrap-admin.test.ts`**

- **happy path**
  - creates user(active) + credential + membership(tenant-admin) on first call
- **idempotency**
  - three consecutive runs yield exactly one admin
- **absent bootstrap config**
  - is a no-op when bootstrap is undefined
  - throws on partial bootstrap (missing email / password / tenant)
- **conflicting state**
  - logs a warning and does not touch a pre-existing user with another status
- **returned outcome**
  - 'not-configured' when there is no bootstrap config
  - 'provisioned' on first run, then 'exists-active'
  - 'exists-non-active' when the existing admin is disabled

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/bootstrap-cli-credentials.test.ts`**

- **happy path**

  - registers the client under the reserved cli-bootstrap handle
- **idempotency**
  - does nothing on a second run once the file exists
- **handle already registered, file missing**
  - logs a warning and does not throw or write a file

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/credentials-store.test.ts`**

- **setCredential + getCredential**

  - upserts on the same (userId, providerId)
  - allows the same userId across different providerIds

- **deleteAllForUser (cascade hook)**

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/csrf.test.ts`**

- **issueCsrfToken**
  - returns a base64url string with at least 43 chars (32 bytes)
  - returns a different value each call
- **verifyCsrfToken**
  - returns true when cookie and header match exactly
  - returns false for different values
  - returns false for empty cookie
  - returns false for empty header
  - returns false when either side is undefined
  - returns false when lengths differ (no early-return timing leak)

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/email-password-provider.test.ts`**

- **contract shape**
  - has id="email-password" and kind="password"
- **happy path**
  - authenticates an active user with correct password
  - canonicalises email input (CD-4)
- **failure paths return identical shape (CD-8)**
  - returns { ok: false, reason: "unknown" } when user does not exist
  - returns { ok: false, reason: "disabled" } for disabled user
  - returns { ok: false, reason: "disabled" } for pending user
  - returns { ok: false, reason: "unknown" } when active user has no email-password credential
  - returns { ok: false, reason: "invalid" } for wrong password
- **constant-time: bcrypt is invoked on every not-ok path (CD-8)**
  - calls bcrypt.compare even when user is missing
  - calls bcrypt.compare even when credential is missing
- **input validation**
  - rejects bad input shape with reason: invalid
- **tenant isolation**
  - does not match a user from a different tenant

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/invites-store.test.ts`**

- **createInvite**
  - returns an opaque base64url token of at least 32 bytes
  - canonicalises email on input (CD-4)
  - does NOT store the plaintext token (only SHA-256 hash)
  - rejects a second active invite for the same (email, tenantId)
  - allows a new invite for the same email in a different tenant
  - allows a new invite after the previous one was consumed
  - allows a new invite after the previous one was revoked
- **findByToken**
  - hashes input and finds the invite
  - returns not_found for an unknown token
  - returns invalid after consume (one-shot)
  - returns invalid after revoke
  - returns invalid for an expired invite even if TTL sweep has not run (CD-9)
- **consume / revoke idempotency**
  - consume on unknown id is a no-op
  - revoke on unknown id is a no-op
  - consuming an already-used invite is a no-op
- **consume atomicity (TOCTOU guard)**
  - returns true for the first consume, false for a repeat
  - returns false for an unknown id
  - returns false for an already-revoked invite
  - exactly one of N parallel consumes wins (compare-and-set)

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/jwt-user.test.ts`**

- **signUserAccessToken / verifyUserAccessToken**
  - round-trips identity claims
  - payload contains NO role / scopes / permissions fields (principle #3)
  - returns null for a tampered token
  - returns null for wrong secret
  - rejects expired tokens
  - rejects a token without `fam` even if signature is valid (CD-2)
  - rejects a token signed with a non-HS256 algorithm (alg pinning)
- **signUserRefreshToken / verifyUserRefreshToken**
  - round-trips identity + family + jti
  - refresh and access tokens are not interchangeable
  - rejects refresh tokens missing `fam`
- **jti is unique per token**
  - different sign calls produce different jti

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/jwt.test.ts`**

  - returns null for tampered token

- **signRefreshToken / verifyRefreshToken**
  - issues a verifiable refresh token
  - returns null for access token passed as refresh
  - returns null for wrong secret

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/password-policy.test.ts`**

**`services/gateway/auth/src/__tests__/sessions-store.test.ts`**

  - throws RefreshNotFoundError for unknown jti
  - throws RefreshExpiredError when refresh is past expiry, and does NOT kill the family
- **rotateRefresh — reuse detection (CD-5)**
  - reusing a consumed jti OUTSIDE the grace window kills the entire family
  - reusing a consumed jti INSIDE the grace window returns the same replacement and keeps family alive

  - removes the family and its refresh tokens; the refresh becomes a not-found, not a reuse

  - revokeAllUserSessionsExcept keeps the named family alive

  - unknown token → unknown_invite, no side-effects

  - aud != clientId → ok:false
  - nonce mismatch → ok:false
  - expired token → ok:false

### `@kb-labs/gateway-contracts`

#### Unit tests

**`services/gateway/contracts/src/__tests__/schemas.test.ts`**

- **GatewayConfigSchema**
  - parses valid config with defaults
  - parses config with upstreams
  - rejects upstream without serviceId
  - rejects upstream prefix without leading slash
- **AuthConfigSchema.providers (ADR-0020 DD-4)**
  - omitting providers leaves it undefined
  - parses a providers map keyed by instance id, each requiring a type
  - passthrough keeps provider-specific keys for the provider schema to validate
  - rejects a provider entry without a type
- **HostRegistrationSchema**
  - parses valid registration
  - accepts all valid capability values
  - rejects invalid capability value
  - requires name
  - requires namespaceId
  - allows empty capabilities array
- **HostDescriptorSchema**
  - parses valid descriptor
  - accepts all status values
  - rejects invalid status
- **AuthContextSchema**
  - parses user auth context
  - rejects invalid tier
  - accepts all type values
- **HelloMessageSchema**
  - parses minimal hello
  - parses hello with reconnect hostId
  - rejects wrong type literal
- **CallMessageSchema**
  - parses valid call
  - parses bulk call
  - requires trace field
- **ErrorMessageSchema**
  - parses valid error
- **SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS**
  - contains 1.0
- **GatewayConfigSchema.access / AuthConfigSchema.enabled**
  - access is optional and its mode is a closed enum
  - auth.enabled is NOT defaulted: mentioning auth must not read as an explicit "enabled: true"
- **AuthConfigSchema.bootstrap.tenantId**
  - is optional: the gateway falls back to the env, then its default

### `@kb-labs/gateway-core`

#### Unit tests

**`services/gateway/core/src/__tests__/adaptive-buffer.test.ts`**

- **AdaptiveBuffer.ttlFor**
  - returns minTTL at full load (1.0)
  - returns minTTL exactly at threshold
  - returns maxTTL at zero load
  - interpolates linearly between min and max
  - clamps to minTTL above threshold
  - respects custom policy
- **AdaptiveBuffer.enqueue**
  - stores a call in cache
  - appends to existing buffer
  - throws HOST_BUFFER_FULL when maxSize exceeded
  - uses adaptive TTL when calling cache.set
- **AdaptiveBuffer.flush**
  - returns buffered calls and clears buffer
  - returns empty array when buffer is empty
  - buffer is empty after flush

**`services/gateway/core/src/__tests__/dispatcher.test.ts`**

- **HostCallDispatcher**
- **registerConnection / connectedCount / firstHost**
  - counts zero initially
  - increments count after register
  - returns first hostId
  - returns undefined firstHost when namespace empty
  - namespaces are isolated
- **removeConnection**
  - decrements count after remove
  - removeConnection on unknown host is a no-op
- **call**
  - sends a call message over the socket
  - rejects immediately if host is not connected
  - resolves with single chunk data on result
  - resolves with all chunks as array when multiple chunks arrive
  - resolves with undefined when result arrives with no chunks
  - rejects on error message with error.message
  - rejects on error message with plain string error
- **handleInbound**
  - ignores messages with no requestId
  - ignores messages for unknown requestId
  - cleans up pending map after resolve

**`services/gateway/core/src/__tests__/gateway-dispatch-transport.test.ts`**

- **GatewayDispatchTransport**
  - POSTs to dispatchEndpoint with correct headers and body
  - sends correct body with adapter:execution, method:execute, args:[request]
  - throws on HTTP 503
  - throws on HTTP 403
  - throws when fetch rejects (network error)
  - returns data from response.result
  - returns data: undefined when result field is absent
- **retryOn503**
  - retries on 503 and succeeds on next attempt
  - throws after exhausting all retry attempts
  - does not retry on 503 when retryOn503 is not set
  - retries correct number of times before success
  - does not retry on other 4xx/5xx errors

**`services/gateway/core/src/__tests__/gateway-host-resolver.test.ts`**

- **GatewayHostResolver**
  - resolves host from Gateway response
  - returns null on 404 (no host found)
  - returns null on 5xx (Gateway error)
  - returns null on network error
  - sends target and namespace in request body
  - defaults namespace to "default"
  - strips trailing slash from gatewayUrl
  - throws on empty internalSecret
  - returns null and logs on invalid response body

**`core/llm-router/test/resolver.test.ts`**

- **TierResolver**
  - should return configured tier for each tier level
- **TierResolver > exact match**
  - should return configured tier when request matches
- **no request (default)**
  - should return configured tier when no tier requested
  - should work with all configured tiers
- **escalation (request < configured)**
  - should escalate small → medium silently
  - should escalate small → large silently
  - should escalate medium → large silently
- **degradation (request > configured)**
  - should degrade large → medium with warning
  - should degrade large → small with warning
  - should degrade medium → small with warning
- **getConfiguredTier**
  - should return the configured tier
- **CapabilityResolver**
  - should return all capabilities from getCapabilities
- **CapabilityResolver > simple config (no capabilities set)**
  - should report all capabilities as available
  - should return true for hasAllCapabilities with any set
- **advanced config (specific capabilities)**
  - should only report configured capabilities as available
  - should return only configured capabilities
  - should correctly check multiple capabilities
  - should work with single capability
- **empty array vs undefined**
  - should treat empty set as "all available"
  - should treat undefined as "all available"

**`core/llm-router/test/router.test.ts`**

- **LLMRouter**
- **basic initialization**
  - should initialize with simple config (no tierMapping)
  - should use legacy tier field if defaultTier not provided
- **tierMapping initialization**
  - should select model from defaultTier on initialization
  - should select highest priority model (lowest priority number)
  - should extract capabilities from tierMapping
- **resolve() with tierMapping**
  - should resolve to correct model for each tier
  - should resolve to default tier when no tier specified
  - should filter by capabilities when specified
  - should fall back to first model if no model matches capabilities
- **ILLM delegation with model injection**
  - should pass selected model to complete()
  - should pass selected model after resolve()
  - should preserve other options when injecting model
  - should not override explicitly provided model
  - should pass model to stream()
- **edge cases**
  - should handle empty tierMapping gracefully
  - should handle missing tier in tierMapping
  - should handle empty array for tier
  - should work without logger
- **chatWithTools support**
  - should report supportsChatWithTools correctly
  - should delegate chatWithTools when available
- **multi-adapter support**
  - should initialize with adapter from default tier
  - should switch adapter when resolving to different tier
  - should load adapter on first complete() call
  - should cache loaded adapters
  - should use correct adapter for each tier
  - should include provider and resource in resolution result
  - should extract provider from new provider field when specified
  - should fall back to default adapter when adapter loader fails
  - should use default adapter when no adapter specified in tierMapping
  - should work without adapterLoader (backward compatible)
  - should handle stream() with multi-adapter
  - should handle chatWithTools() with multi-adapter

### `@kb-labs/mind-core`

#### E2E / journey tests

**`plugins/mind/core/src/answer/explore.spec.ts`**

- **toExploreEntries**
  - dedupes by file, keeping the first (best-ranked) occurrence
  - derives `why` from the matched line, not from rules
  - carries matchedBy and stale through
- **spreadOf**
  - counts distinct parent directories
  - treats root-level files as one directory
- **orientationSummary**
  - returns empty string when LLM is disabled
  - returns empty string when there are no entries
  - returns the synthesized text when enabled with entries

**`plugins/mind/core/src/answer/field-check.spec.ts`**

- **field-check (anti-hallucination)**
  - extracts code-like symbols, ignores prose words
  - rate 1 + no ungrounded when every symbol is in the sources
  - flags a fabricated symbol as ungrounded and drops the rate
  - grounds dotted members by their head object
  - no symbols → rate 1 (nothing to verify)

**`plugins/mind/core/src/answer/verify.spec.ts`**

- **verifySources**
  - rate is 1 when files exist and snippets are present
  - drops to file-weight only when the snippet is gone (stale index)
  - is 0 when the file no longer exists (hallucinated/deleted source)
  - empty input verifies vacuously
- **computeConfidence**
  - multiplies retrieval confidence by verification rate
  - warns when below the floor
  - no warning at or above the floor
  - clamps to [0,1]

**`plugins/mind/core/src/drop.spec.ts`**

- **mind drop — missing index guard**
  - throws for an index that has no manifest (does not fake success)
  - drops vectors + manifest for an existing index

**`plugins/mind/core/src/ingest/chunk.spec.ts`**

- **slidingWindowChunks**
  - produces a single chunk for short content
  - splits long content into multiple line-aligned chunks
  - derives kind from path
  - skips empty/whitespace-only content
  - terminates and covers all lines even with large overlap

**`plugins/mind/core/src/ingest/embed.spec.ts`**

- **embedChunks — batching bounded by count + token budget**
  - caps a batch at 96 chunks for small inputs
  - splits by token budget when inputs are large (never one oversized request)
  - truncates a single oversized input to the per-input char cap

**`plugins/mind/core/src/ingest/structural.spec.ts`**

- **structuralChunks**
  - splits a code file at top-level declaration boundaries
  - falls back to sliding window when there are no boundaries
  - chunkFile uses structural only for code when ast=true
  - chunkFile never uses structural for docs

**`plugins/mind/core/src/pipeline.spec.ts`**

- **Tracer**
  - runs a stage and records its trace
  - threads multiple stages and preserves order
  - omits outputCount for non-array stage output

**`plugins/mind/core/src/retrieval/bm25.spec.ts`**

- **tokenize**
  - splits camelCase and snake_case identifiers
  - drops single-character tokens
- **bm25Search**
  - ranks the most relevant chunk first
  - returns only matching chunks (score > 0)
  - returns nothing for a non-matching query
  - respects the limit
  - handles an empty corpus

**`plugins/mind/core/src/retrieval/expand.spec.ts`**

- **expandQuery**
  - appends the LLM-suggested terms after the raw query
  - falls back to the raw query when the LLM returns nothing
  - falls back to the raw query when the LLM throws
- **retrieval — query expansion wiring**
  - on: the expanded term produces a lexical hit the raw query could not
  - off: no lexical hit from the (unmatched) raw query alone

**`plugins/mind/core/src/retrieval/fuse.spec.ts`**

- **rrfFuse — provenance (matchedBy)**
  - labels results by which list(s) surfaced them
  - unlabelled lists resolve to "both" (neutral)

**`plugins/mind/core/src/retrieval/hyde.spec.ts`**

- **retrieval — HyDE (hypothetical document embeddings)**
  - on: feeds the LLM hypothetical (not the bare query) to the vector embedder
  - off: feeds only the raw query — never the hypothetical

**`plugins/mind/core/src/retrieval/quality.spec.ts`**

- **rerank**
  - boosts chunks that cover more query terms
  - returns input unchanged for an empty query
- **dedupRanked**
  - drops near-duplicate chunks, keeping the higher-ranked one
  - keeps distinct chunks

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/benchmarks/ab.spec.ts`**

- **benchmark A/B gate**
  - reports per-metric deltas and a verdict
  - dedup ON must not regress quality vs OFF
  - detects a deliberate regression (sanity: tiny limit hurts recall)
  - exposes a per-group breakdown

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/benchmarks/eval.spec.ts`**

- **benchmark — regression gate**
  - meets or exceeds the frozen baseline on every metric
  - every non-abstain query retrieves its relevant file in the top 5

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/integration/ask.spec.ts`**

- **mind.ask — agent answers (frozen agent-response-v1)**
  - returns a contract-valid response in instant mode (no LLM)
  - uses the LLM answer in auto mode when available
  - abstains gracefully on an empty index (low confidence, no sources)
  - records the query in feedback history

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/integration/incremental.spec.ts`**

- **ingest — incremental (hash-delta) indexing**
  - first index treats all files as added
  - re-index with no changes embeds nothing (all unchanged)
  - only the changed file is re-embedded
  - a new file is added incrementally
  - a deleted file is pruned from the index
  - --full rebuilds everything regardless of hashes

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/integration/ingest-progress.spec.ts`**

- **ingest — staged progress events**
  - emits ordered stages with an embedding counter on a fresh index
  - re-index with no changes reports an empty delta and embeds nothing

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/integration/search.spec.ts`**

- **mind facade — index + search (vertical slice)**
  - indexes files into an index and reports counts
  - search finds the relevant file
  - isolates indexes: searching one corpus never returns another’s chunks
  - status reports per-index summaries
  - re-indexing replaces rather than duplicates
  - defaults the index id from config when omitted

**`plugins/mind/core/tests/integration/sync.spec.ts`**

- **mind — incremental sync**
  - sync add brings a new document into the index
  - sync delete removes a document and its chunks
  - sync update replaces a document’s chunks (no duplication)
  - sync list reflects synced documents

**`core/plugin-runtime/src/platform/__tests__/assembly-chain.test.ts`**

- **assemblePlatform — LLM chain structure**
  - AnalyticsLLM is the outermost wrapper when no router config is given
  - QueuedLLM is in the chain — broker.enqueue is called for complete()
  - includes LLMRouter as outermost (before PII) when config.llm is provided
  - includes PIIRedactionLLM as outermost when privacy config is provided
  - skips LLMRouter when config.llm is absent
  - skips PIIRedactionLLM when _privacy is absent from config
  - skips PIIRedactionLLM when _privacy.enabled is false
- **assemblePlatform — embeddings chain structure**
  - AnalyticsEmbeddings is the outermost wrapper
  - QueuedEmbeddings is in the chain — broker.enqueue is called for embed()
- **assemblePlatform — vectorStore chain structure**
  - AnalyticsVectorStore is the outermost wrapper
  - QueuedVectorStore is in the chain — broker.enqueue is called for search()
- **assemblePlatform — cache chain structure**
  - includes AnalyticsCache (no queuing for cache)
- **assemblePlatform — storage chain structure**
  - includes AnalyticsStorage (no queuing for storage)
- **assemblePlatform — pass-through adapters**
  - analytics adapter is unchanged (pass-through strategy)
  - eventBus adapter is unchanged (pass-through strategy)
  - logger adapter is unchanged by assemblePlatform (governance wraps per-plugin)
- **assemblePlatform — safety invariants**
  - returns a new object, does not mutate raw platform
  - skips adapters that are undefined in raw platform

**`core/plugin-runtime/src/platform/__tests__/call-chain.test.ts`**

- **call-chain: complete() path**
  - complete() calls raw.complete exactly once
  - complete() returns the raw adapter response
  - analytics.track IS called for complete() — fails before pipeline.ts fix
  - analytics.track called with llm.completion.started event
  - analytics.track called with llm.completion.completed event
  - raw.complete called exactly once — no double invocation
  - analytics.track called exactly once per event type — no double wrapping
- **call-chain: stream() path**
  - stream() produces chunks from raw adapter
  - analytics.track called for stream() (AnalyticsLLM wraps stream path)
- **call-chain: broker enqueue mechanics**
  - executor captures the adapter as registered, not as assembled — root cause of the bug

**`core/plugin-runtime/src/platform/__tests__/database-governance.test.ts`**

- **wrapDocumentDatabase**
- **deny stub when permission is absent**
  - rejects every operation with PermissionError
  - still allows ping() and close() so health-checks survive
  - findStream throws on iteration, not on call
- **name validation**
  - rejects collection names containing the namespace separator
  - rejects empty collection name in owns
  - rejects duplicate names in owns
  - rejects access entry whose collection clashes with owns
  - rejects access entry with empty ops
- **owned collections**
  - routes a read on an owned collection to <pluginId>__<name>
  - routes a write on an owned collection to <pluginId>__<name>
  - denies reads on a collection NOT in owns or access
  - denies ensureCollection on owned collection without ddl.ownCollections
  - allows ensureCollection when ddl.ownCollections is true
- **cross-plugin access grants**
  - allows a granted read into another plugin namespace
  - denies a write on a read-only grant
  - allows write when grant includes write op
  - forbids DDL on a foreign-owned collection regardless of grant
- **namespace isolation**
  - two plugins with the same collection name see disjoint data
  - a plugin cannot read another plugin\
- **transactions**
  - enforces the same governance inside transaction()
  - commits a namespaced write performed inside a transaction
- **bulkWrite**
  - namespaces every op in the batch and enforces write permission once
  - denies bulkWrite when collection has read-only grant
- **wrapKVStore**
- **deny stub when permission is absent**
  - rejects every operation
  - still allows ping/close
- **namespacing**
  - prefixes keys with <pluginId>: under the hood
  - two plugins cannot see each other\
  - scan only yields keys from the calling plugin\
  - scan respects a prefix argument relative to the plugin namespace
  - getMany namespaces every key
- **atomic ops respect namespace**
  - cas operates on the namespaced key
  - incr operates on the namespaced key
  - expire/persist/ttl operate on the namespaced key
- **input validation**
  - rejects empty key
- **validateDatabaseGrants**
  - passes a plugin with no access grants
  - passes when grant matches an export
  - fails when the owner plugin is not installed
  - fails when the owner does not export the requested collection
  - fails when requested ops exceed what the owner exports
  - allows multi-op grants when the export covers both
  - rejects self-grants (owner = candidate.id)
  - reports multiple errors when several grants are broken

  - allows all keys when permission is true

  - enforces namespace prefix on delete
  - enforces namespace prefix on zadd
  - enforces namespace prefix on zrangebyscore
  - enforces namespace prefix on zrem

- **wrapStorage**
  - denies all methods when no storage permission
  - allows all paths when permission is true
  - enforces path restriction on read
  - enforces path restriction on write
  - enforces path restriction on delete
  - enforces path restriction on exists
  - enforces path restriction on list
- **wrapVectorStore**
  - denies all methods when no vectorStore permission
  - allows all operations when permission is true

  - does not double-prefix ids that already start with namespace
  - filters search results by namespace metadata and strips the id prefix

- **wrapEmbeddings**
  - denies when no embeddings permission
  - allows embed when permission is true
  - allows embedBatch when permission is true
- **wrapNotifier**
  - denies notify and subscribe when no notifier permission
  - denies notify when emit is false
  - allows notify when emit is true, stamps source as pluginId
  - denies subscribe when no subscribe permission
  - filters events by allowed source
  - allows all sources when sources contains wildcard

  - rejects a stale plan whose versions no longer match the working tree
  - rejects a pre-bump plan when the bump was already applied (and vice versa)

  - forwards the caller channel into a fresh replan of a stale artifact
  - rejects a plan belonging to a different channel

**`plugins/release/manager-core/src/__tests__/checks.test.ts`**

- **runReleaseChecks — parser: exitcode**
  - passes when command exits 0
  - fails when command exits non-zero
- **runReleaseChecks — parser: json**
  - passes when stdout contains { ok: true }
  - passes when stdout contains { success: true }
  - passes when stdout contains { status: "ok" }
  - fails when json reports { ok: false }
  - fails when stdout is not valid JSON
- **runReleaseChecks — parser: function**
  - uses custom parser function
  - custom parser receives stdout, stderr, exitCode
- **runReleaseChecks — optional**
  - continues after optional failure
  - runs every later check after a required failure (fail-late)
- **runReleaseChecks — fail-late with blocking/dependsOn**
  - runs all checks even when an earlier non-blocking check failed
  - skips a dependent of a failed blocking check with an explicit reason
  - does not skip dependents when the dependency is not blocking
  - does not skip dependents when the dependency passed
  - an optional failing check never blocks, even if marked blocking
  - skips a perPackage dependent only for packages whose blocking dependency failed
  - skips the whole perPackage dependent when every package failed the dependency
  - overall exit semantics: fails if any non-optional check failed or was skipped
- **runReleaseChecks — governed timeout diagnostics**
  - surfaces partial stdout/stderr captured before the kill, after retry is exhausted
  - does not fabricate stdout/stderr fields for a non-governed rejection

**`plugins/release/manager-core/src/__tests__/pack-verify.test.ts`**

- **pack-static (batch, real tar + node --check)**
  - passes a healthy tarball and reports every failing package with classifiable text
  - fails a package that has no staged tarball instead of silently passing it
  - collects main/module/types/bin/exports leaves and skips globs
- **pack-install (aggregated)**
  - installs everything in ONE consumer from the staging registry and imports once
  - skips import() for app/daemon packages (declared list, bin, name) but still resolves them
  - reports a failing import per package
  - bisects a failed aggregated install down to the offending package
  - names every uninstallable package when several fail
  - attributes an interaction failure to the whole subset when halves install alone
  - stops bisecting when the install budget is exhausted and says so
  - runs isolated installs only for configured and changed packages
  - a package failing only in isolation is reported
  - installs tarball paths when no staging registry is known
  - turns an install that outlives its budget into a failure that classifies as a timeout
- **runImportPass (real node process, fixture consumer)**
  - imports libraries, resolves apps without executing them, and isolates a crashing package
- **resolveStagedArtifacts**
  - reuses tarballs from the Stage manifest without packing or fetching
  - falls back to downloading the same tarball from the staging registry
  - leaves missing packages unresolved so the checks can name them
- **runReleaseChecks with builtin pack checks**
  - skips pack-install only for packages that failed the blocking pack-static, keeps phases and report rows
  - does not run disabled checks unless requested with `only`
  - fails clearly when no staging information is available
- **pack-static: undeclared imports**
  - fails an import of a package that is not declared, naming package, file and specifier
  - treats devDependencies-only as undeclared
  - accepts builtins, node: URLs, self and relative imports, declared deps/peers/optional and subpaths
  - detects dynamic import() and require() in ESM and CJS files, and export ... from
  - honours undeclaredImportAllowlist (plain name and package:name)
  - is invisible to the aggregated install but caught by pack-static (hoisting hides the missing dependency)
- **real Arborist in-process install (offline, dependency-free tarballs)**
  - installs two tarballs into one consumer and imports them in one pass
  - surfaces a real install error (workspace: dependency) and bisects to the offending package

**`plugins/release/manager-core/src/__tests__/preflight.test.ts`**

- **runReleasePreflight**
  - all green: ok, every check passed, baseline skipped without a source
  - baseline check is skipped when no source is provided
  - wrong branch -> KB_RELEASE_BRANCH_NOT_MASTER naming both branches
  - dirty tree -> KB_RELEASE_TREE_DIRTY with count
  - only tool-generated .kb dirt -> passes with a warning and the count
  - a source file change fails and names it
  - mixed dirt fails listing only blocking paths, capped at 10
  - docker down -> KB_RELEASE_DOCKER_UNAVAILABLE
  - docker binary missing (exec throws) still yields a coded failure, not a crash
  - staging registry unreachable -> exact kb-dev command with the net offset, nothing started
  - npm registry unreachable (HTTP 503) -> REGISTRY_UNREACHABLE with target=npm
  - no token and gh not logged in -> KB_RELEASE_TOKEN_MISSING
  - gh login alone satisfies auth; env token never reaches the output
  - git tag vs npm mismatch -> KB_RELEASE_BASELINE_DRIFT
  - packages disagreeing on the stable dist-tag -> BASELINE_DRIFT
  - unresolved npm baseline or a throwing baseline is skipped, not a false drift
  - reports every failure at once and all are environment-classified with envelope fields
- **resolveStagingRegistry**
  - prefers explicit, then env, then 4873 + offset

- **CSRF header**
  - adds X-CSRF-Token on POST
  - adds X-CSRF-Token on PUT, PATCH, DELETE
  - does NOT add X-CSRF-Token on GET
  - does NOT add X-CSRF-Token when cookie is absent
- **credentials: include**
  - always sends credentials: include
- **401 → single-flight refresh → retry**
  - refreshes on 401 and retries the original request
  - single-flight: two parallel 401s make only one refresh call
  - calls onUnauthenticated and does not retry when refresh fails
  - propagates non-401 errors without refreshing
- **multi-tab BroadcastChannel coordination**
  - waits for refresh:done from another tab and does NOT call /auth/refresh itself
  - falls back to own refresh after claim window expires with no cross-tab signal
- **baseURL prepended**
  - prepends baseURL to the path

- **aggregated daily costs**
  - should format daily totals
  - should format typical production daily costs
- **edge cases**
  - should handle zero cost
  - should handle very tiny costs (scientific notation)
  - should handle large costs
- **precision thresholds**
  - should use 2 decimals for >= $1
  - should use 4 decimals for >= $0.01
  - should use 6 decimals for >= $0.0001
  - should use scientific notation for < $0.0001
- **what we expect to see in the chart tooltip**
  - should show readable values for typical daily aggregates
  - should NOT show $0.00 for small but non-zero values
- **real world scenario: what chart shows on Jan 16**
  - should handle sum of all LLM calls on Jan 16
  - should handle very low daily total (few requests)
