### `@kb-labs/workflow-constants`

#### Unit tests

**`plugins/workflow/constants/src/__tests__/state-machine.test.ts`**

- **state-machine transition tables**
  - ${kind}: every state in the enum has a table entry
  - ${kind}: every legal "to" target is itself a known state
  - legal transitions do not throw
  - illegal transitions throw IllegalStateTransitionError
  - same-state transitions are always a no-op (idempotent re-write)
  - allowReset bypasses the table for bulk-reset callers
  - cancellation stays legal from parked job states (required for cancelRun)
- **isTerminal / isParked / isActive predicates**
  - job: waiting_approval and waiting_child are parked, not terminal, not active
  - job: interrupted is parked, not terminal (resumable — §0E)
  - job: running/queued are active, not parked, not terminal
  - job: success/failed/cancelled/skipped are terminal, not active, not parked
  - every job state is classified as exactly one of terminal/parked/active
  - every run state is classified as exactly one of terminal/active (runs have no parked state)
  - every step state is classified as exactly one of terminal/parked/active
- **IllegalStateTransitionError**
  - carries kind/from/to for callers to inspect

**`plugins/workflow/runtime/src/runners/__tests__/output-normalizer.test.ts`**

- **toWorkflowOutputs**
  - passes through nested objects
- **toWorkflowOutputs > object handler output (workflow handlers)**
  - passes through plain objects as-is
  - passes through arrays as objects
- **CommandResult handler output (CLI commands via command: steps)**
  - extracts .result from CommandResult with object payload
  - extracts .result from CommandResult with meta
  - wraps primitive .result in { result }
  - wraps numeric .result in { result }
  - returns {} when CommandResult.result is undefined (key present)
  - returns {} when CommandResult.result is explicitly undefined
  - handles failed CommandResult (still extracts result)
  - handles CommandResult with null .result
- **primitive handler output**
  - wraps string in { result }
  - wraps number in { result }
  - wraps boolean in { result }
  - wraps false in { result }
  - wraps zero in { result }
  - wraps empty string in { result }
- **void / null / undefined handler output**
  - returns {} for undefined
  - returns {} for null
- **consistency guarantee: in-process vs subprocess produce same outputs**
  - workflow handler returning object → same outputs in both modes
  - CLI handler returning CommandResult → same outputs in both modes
- **edge cases**
  - passes through shell handler output as-is (has exitCode but no result key)
  - passes through Date objects

### `@kb-labs/workflow-steps`

#### Unit tests

**`plugins/workflow/steps/src/__tests__/approval.test.ts`**

- **ApprovalInput type shape**

  - accepts a fully specified ApprovalInput
  - accepts arbitrary values in context
  - has correct field types

  - represents an approved outcome
  - represents a rejected outcome
  - accepts extra data from the approver via index signature
  - has correct types for required fields

**`plugins/workflow/steps/src/__tests__/gate.test.ts`**

- **GateHandler restart-target guard**
  - FAILS when restartFrom matches neither a step nor a job the worker can reset
  - RESTARTS when restartFrom matches a valid target (step in this job OR a job by name)
  - skips the guard when no targets are supplied (backward compatible)
- **GateInput type shape**
  - accepts a minimal GateInput with required fields only
  - accepts a fully specified GateInput
  - accepts a restartFrom route action with optional context
  - has correct field types
- **GateOutput type shape**
  - represents a continue outcome
  - represents a fail outcome
  - represents a restart outcome with restartFrom
  - accepts any value type for decisionValue
  - has correct field types
  - represents a skip outcome with skipTo
- **GateHandler skipTo**
  - returns skip action when decision matches a skipTo route
  - returns continue when decision does not match skipTo route (default: continue)
  - FAILS when skipTo target is not in validRestartTargets
  - allows skipTo when no validRestartTargets supplied (backward compatible)
- **GateRouteAction type variants**
  - accepts string literal "continue"
  - accepts string literal "fail"
  - accepts an object with restartFrom
  - accepts an object with restartFrom and context
  - accepts an object with skipTo

**`plugins/workflow/steps/src/__tests__/shell.test.ts`**

- **parseOutputMarkerLine**
  - returns null for a line with no marker
  - parses a plain ::kb-output:: marker
  - throws a descriptive error for malformed JSON in plain marker (F6)
  - throws a descriptive error when plain marker JSON is an array (not object)
  - parses ::kb-output:base64:: with valid base64-encoded JSON (F7)
  - handles multi-line text safely via base64 — the F7 fix
  - throws on invalid base64 in base64 marker
  - throws when base64 decodes to non-object JSON
  - base64 marker takes priority when both markers appear on the same line
- **mergeJsonOutputs — ::kb-output:: marker**
  - extracts outputs from ::kb-output:: marker line
  - works with marker as the only line
  - extracts from marker even with pnpm noise before it
  - merges multiple marker lines
  - later marker overrides earlier for same key
  - calls warn callback and skips key on malformed marker JSON (F6 fix)
  - without warn callback, malformed marker is silently skipped (no throw)
  - ignores marker with array JSON and calls warn
  - preserves base ShellOutput fields
  - marker takes priority over fallback JSON parse
- **mergeJsonOutputs — ::kb-output:base64:: marker**
  - extracts outputs from a base64-encoded marker
  - handles multi-line text values safely (F7 regression guard)
  - handles special chars and unicode safely
  - merges multiple base64 markers
  - calls warn on invalid base64 and continues processing other lines
  - base64 and plain markers can coexist in the same stdout
- **mergeJsonOutputs — fallback (backward compat)**
  - merges a flat JSON object from stdout into the output record
  - preserves the base ShellOutput fields even when merging
  - ignores invalid JSON and returns the base output unchanged
  - ignores partial / malformed JSON and returns the base output unchanged
  - does NOT merge JSON arrays — only plain objects are merged
  - does NOT merge primitive JSON values (string, number, boolean)
  - returns base output unchanged when stdout is empty
  - returns base output unchanged when stdout is only whitespace
  - merges nested objects from JSON stdout
  - handles stdout with leading/trailing whitespace around valid JSON
  - works correctly when command fails (exitCode != 0)
- **ShellInput type shape**
  - accepts a minimal input with only command
  - accepts a fully specified input
- **ShellOutput type shape**
  - has the expected fields
- **shellHandler — timeout regression (BUG: timedOut + reject:false = silent success)**
  - throws an error when the command times out — must not return ok:true with empty stdout
  - keeps successful stderr diagnostics at warn level
  - includes the actual stderr in the failure log entry, not just its shape
  - includes whatever the command printed before being killed in the timeout log entry
  - carries the captured output tail in the thrown error itself, not just the log entry
- **tail**
  - returns the text unchanged when under the limit
  - keeps the END of the text, not the start — the actionable error is usually last
  - marks truncated output so it is not mistaken for the full log
  - surfaces a ::kb-output:: line even when a trailing wrapper banner would otherwise push it out of the tail
  - surfaces a ::kb-output:base64:: line the same way
  - does not duplicate the marker line when it already falls inside the raw tail window
  - truncates an overlong marker line instead of blowing the whole budget on it
- **LineBatcher**
  - does not flush before maxLines or maxMs is reached
  - flushes exactly once when maxLines lines arrive, not once per line
  - flushes on the time window even if maxLines was never reached
  - starts a fresh batch (and timer) after a flush
  - manual flush() is a no-op when nothing is buffered
  - manual flush() emits whatever is buffered and cancels the pending timer
- **shellHandler — durable per-line log persistence (batched)**
  - persists a small step's stdout as one batched logger call, not one per line
  - splits more than one batch worth of lines (>50) into multiple logger calls
  - routes stderr batches through logger.warn, matching the per-line level
